Using model llama-3.3-70b-versatile for 1377.25 tokens (approx)
LLM Response: SELECT 
    e.CT_Num, 
    c.CT_Intitule, 
    COUNT(e.DO_Piece) AS nb_commandes
FROM 
    F_DOCENTETE e
JOIN 
    F_COMPTET c ON e.CT_Num = c.CT_Num
WHERE 
    e.DO_Type = 6 AND e.DO_Domaine = 0 AND c.CT_Type = 0
GROUP BY 
    e.CT_Num, c.CT_Intitule
HAVING 
    COUNT(e.DO_Piece) > 5
ORDER BY 
    nb_commandes DESC;

GROUP BY

HAVING 
    COUNT(e.DO_Piece) > 5
ORDER BY

ORDER BY

LLM Response: ```sql
SELECT c.CT_Num, c.CT_Intitule, COUNT(e.DO_Piece) AS nb_factures_impayees
FROM F_DOCENTETE e
LEFT JOIN F_COMPTET c ON e.CT_Num = c.CT_Num
LEFT JOIN reglements r ON e.DO_Piece = r.DO_Piece
WHERE e.DO_Type = 3 AND e.DO_Domaine = 0 AND r.DO_Piece IS NULL
GROUP BY c.CT_Num
HAVING COUNT(e.DO_Piece) > 3
ORDER BY nb_factures_impayees DESC
```

GROUP BY c.CT_Num

ORDER BY nb_factures_impayees DESC

```sql
WITH 
ca_annee_precedente AS (

  FROM F_DOCENTETE e 
  JOIN F_DOCLIGNE l ON e.DO_Piece=l.DO_Piece

  GROUP BY e.CT_Num
),
ca_annee_courante AS (
  SELECT e.CT_Num, SUM(l.DL_Qte*l.DL_PrixUnitaire) AS ca_annee_courante
  FROM F_DOCENTETE e 
  JOIN F_DOCLIGNE l ON e.DO_Piece=l.DO_Piece 
  WHERE e.DO_Type=3 AND e.DO_Domaine=0 AND e.DO_Date >= (SELECT DATE('now', '-1 year'))
  GROUP BY e.CT_Num
)
SELECT c.CT_Num, c.CT_Intitule, ap.ca_annee_precedente, ac.ca_annee_courante
FROM F_COMPTET c
JOIN ca_annee_precedente ap ON c.CT_Num = ap.CT_Num
JOIN ca_annee_courante ac ON c.CT_Num = ac.CT_Num
WHERE ap.ca_annee_precedente > ac.ca_annee_courante
```
